5660e583e07e0be9.js:2 Cache data may be lost when replacing the sharedSecretLinks field of a Repl object.

This could cause additional (usually avoidable) network requests to fetch data that were otherwise cached.

To address this problem (which is not a bug in Apollo Client), define a custom merge function for the Repl.sharedSecretLinks field, so InMemoryCache can safely merge these objects:

  existing: [{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:c20ae211-ac23-4b1f-a8ed-119bf9c0e692"},{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:945daa39-573d-4920-af8b-eb859bce95e1"},{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:69a945ba-2912-44e8-bb1b-bc3d53f47667"},{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:4f4c7949-2d91-46a5-a693-5a137e4da379"}]
  incoming: [{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:c20ae211-ac23-4b1f-a8ed-119bf9c0e692"},{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:945daa39-573d-4920-af8b-eb859bce95e1"},{"__ref":"SharedSecretLink:69a945ba-2912-44e8-bb1b-bc3d53f47667"}]
